    ABOUT RADIANCE                                                                      關於金輝：
    Radiance Holdings (Group) Company Limited (stock code: 9993.HK) is a                金輝控股（集團）有限公司（股票代碼：9993.HK ）是一家全國
    reputable large property developer with national presence, regional focus and       佈局、區域聚焦、城市領先且聲譽卓著的大型地產開發商，
    leading positions in select cities, and we focus on providing quality residential   專注於為首次購房者及首次改善型購房者提供優質的住宅
    properties to first-time homebuyers and first-time upgraders. With over 27          物 業。憑 借 逾27年 的 經 驗，我 們 已 將 業 務 擴 展 至 中 國 八 大
    years’ experience, we have expanded our operations into eight regions with          經濟發展勢頭強勁的區域，包括長三角、環渤海、深惠、東
    strong growth potential in China, namely, the Yangtze River Delta, the Bohai        南、華東、華中、西南及西北。
    Economic Rim, Shenzhen/Huizhou, Southeastern China, Eastern China, Central
    China, Southwestern China and Northwestern China.

    In terms of comprehensive strengths, we were ranked as one of “China’s              按 綜 合 實 力 計，我 們 自2014年 起 連 續10年 獲 中 國 房 地 產 協
    Top 50 Real Estate Developers” (「中國房地產企業綜合前50強」) by the                             會 及 上 海 易 居 房 地 產 研 究 院 中 國 房 地 產 測 評 中 心 評 為「中
    China Real Estate Association（中國房地產協會）and the China Real Estate                     國房地產企業綜合前50強」；並榮獲「 2023房地產上市公司
    Appraisal Center of Shanghai E-house China Research Institute（上海易居房                 綜合實力第23強」等榮譽。
    地產研究院）for ten consecutive years since 2014 and was named “2023 Top
    23 Comprehensive Strength of Real Estate Listed Companies”.

MAJOR EVENTS OF RADIANCE                                                          金輝大事記
March 2024                                                                        2024年3月
Radiance Holdings has zeroed out all credit-type debts, marking an                金輝控股清零了信用債，這標誌著公司在債務管理方面邁
important step in debt management and demonstrating the Company’s                 出 了 重 要 一 步，體 現 了 其 恪 守 財 務 底 線、優 化 資 本 結 構 的
determination to scrupulously adhering to the financial bottom line and           決 心。這 一 舉 措 不 僅 有 助 於 降 低 財 務 風 險，還 增 強 了 企 業
optimize its capital structure. This initiative not only helps to reduce          的財務靈活性，為未來的業務拓展提供了更為堅實的財務
financial risks, but also enhances the financial flexibility of the enterprise,   基礎。
providing a more solid financial foundation for future business expansion.

April 2024                                                                        2024年4月
On 15 April, Radiance Holdings completed the payment of its US dollar             4月15日，金輝控股完成了美元債的兌付，結清了所有公開
bonds and settled all credit bonds issued in the open market. Such action         市場發行的信用類債券。這一行動進一步鞏固了公司的財
further consolidated the Company’s financial stability, circulating a positive    務穩健性，向市場傳遞了金輝控股在復雜經濟環境下依然
signal in the market that Radiance Holdings could maintain its financial          能夠保持財務承諾和償債能力的積極信號。這不僅有助於
commitment and solvency in a complex economic environment. This not               提升投資者的信心，也為公司在資本市場上贏得了更高的
only helps to boost investor confidence, but also earns the Company a             信譽。
higher reputation in the capital market.

May 2024                                                                          2024年5月

                                                                                                       ，以5個100行動綱
delivery system, promoting the enhancement of product power with five             領，以可量化產品交付體系，多維度、全過程地助推產品力
100 action programs and quantifiable product delivery system in a multi-          的提升，實現交付前、中、後的百分保障，回應每一位居者
dimensional and full-process manner, achieving 100% guarantee before,             的幸福當下。
during and after delivery, and responded to the happiness of moments of
every home owner.

On 24 May, Sofitel Shanghai North Bund officially commenced business              5月24日，上海北外灘金輝索菲特酒店正式開業，上海北外
operation. The opening of Sofitel Shanghai North Bund not only adds a             灘金輝索菲特酒店的開業不僅為北外灘地區增添了一張璀
sparkling new landmark to the North Bund, but also injects new vitality           璨的新名片，也為上海的高端酒店市場註入了新的活力。
into the high-end hotel market in Shanghai.

July 2024                                                                         2024年7月
On 12 July, Radiance Holdings successfully issued RMB550 million medium-          7月12日成功發行5.5億元中期票據，這不僅是對其財務穩健
term notes, which not only recognized our financial stability and market          性和市場認可度的肯定，也為公司未來的發展提供了堅實

August 2024                                                                       2024年8月
On 23 August, Xi’an Radiance Plaza D16 Creative Street officially                 8月23日 西 安 金 輝 廣 場D16創 意 街 區 正 式 開 業，作 為曲江二

Qujiang Phase II and the Aerospace Base, D16 makes up for the lack of             化社交目的地」的短板，與金輝廣場一期形成互補（一期主
“personalized social destination” in the region and complements Radiance          打家庭消費，D16側重年輕潮流）        ，共同構建「 MALL+街區+運

4.       Weighted average cost of indebtedness represents the weighted average of                   4.    加權平均債務成本是各項未償還債務的利息成本之加權平
         interest costs of all outstanding indebtedness.                                                  均。

5.       Net gearing ratio is calculated by dividing total borrowings (including current            5.    淨負債率是按借貸總額（包括即期及長期計息銀行及其他借
         and long-term interest-bearing bank and other borrowings, current and long-                      款、即期及長期從資產抵押證券獲得的所得款項、即期及長
         term proceeds from asset-backed securities, current and long-term senior                         期 優 先 票 據 及 即 期 及 長 期 公 司 債 券）減 現 金 及 銀 行 餘 額（包
         notes as well as current and long-term corporate bonds) minus cash and bank                      括受限制現金、已抵押存款及現金及現金等價物）再除以權
         balances (including restricted cash, pledged deposits as well as cash and cash                   益總額計算。
         equivalents) by total equity.

6.       Liability asset ratio after excluding receipts in advance is calculated by dividing        6.    剔除預收款項後的資產負債率是按負債總額減合同負債再
         total liabilities minus contract liabilities by total assets minus contract liabilities.         除以資產總額減合同負債計算。

Management Consulting Services                                                    管理諮詢服務
The Group provides management consulting services to its joint ventures           本集團向合營企業及聯營公司提供管理諮詢服務，相關服
and associates, such services mainly include management consultation              務主要包括於物業開發及銷售過程中向該等實體就建設、
services provided to these entities in connection with the construction,          物業銷售及營銷以及整體項目管理提供管理諮詢服務。管
sales and marketing of properties, and overall project management during          理諮詢服務所得收入由截至二零二三年十二月三十一日止
the development and sales of properties. Our revenue from management              年度約人民幣17.5百萬元減少約16%至截至二零二四年十二
consulting services decreased by approximately 16% to approximately               月三十一日止年度約人民幣14.7百萬元，主要是由於我們所
RMB14.7 million for the year ended 31 December 2024 from approximately            提供管理諮詢服務的合營企業及聯營公司開發的項目規模
RMB17.5 million for the year ended 31 December 2023, which was mainly             有所縮少。
due the decrease in scale of projects developed by our joint ventures and
associates to which we provided management consulting services.

amounting to RMB71.4 million from the provision of hotel services.                本集團帶來收入人民幣71.4百萬元。

Cost of Sales                                                                     銷售成本
Cost of property development and sales mainly consists of construction            物業開發及銷售成本主要包括建設成本、土地收購成本及
costs, land acquisition costs and capitalized interest. During the year           資 本 化 利 息。截 至 二 零 二 四 年 十 二 月 三 十 一 日 止 年 度，銷
ended 31 December 2024, our cost of sales amounted to approximately               售成本約為人民幣30,565.3百萬元，較截至二零二三年十二
RMB30,565.3 million, representing a decrease of 3.6% as compared to that of       月三十一日止年度為人民幣31,720百萬元減少3.6%，主要歸
RMB31,720 million for the year ended 31 December 2023, which was primarily        因於本集團已交付總建築面積減少及截至二零二四年十二
attributable to a decrease in the total GFA delivered and the increase in the     月三十一日止年度本集團的存貨減值撥備增加所致。
Group’s provision for impairment of inventories during the year ended 31
December 2024.

Gross Loss and Gross Profit Margin                                                毛損及毛利率
As a result of the foregoing, for the year ended 31 December 2024, the Group      基 於 上 文 所 述，截 至 二 零 二 四 年 十 二 月 三 十 一 日 止 年 度，
recorded gross loss of approximately RMB5,799.2 million, as compared to           本 集 團 錄 得 毛 損 約 人 民 幣5,799.2百 萬 元，而 截 至 二 零 二 三

Financial Risk                                                                        財務風險
The Group’s businesses exposed us to various financial risks, including               本 集 團 的 業 務 使 其 承 受 多 種 財 務 風 險，包 括 利 率 風 險、外
interest rate risk, foreign exchange risk, credit risk and liquidity risk. In order   幣 風 險、信 貸 風 險 及 流 動 資 金 風 險。為 將 本 集 團 所 面 臨 的
to minimize such risk exposures of the Group, we do not use any derivatives           該等風險保持最低，本集團並無使用任何衍生及其他工具
and other instruments for hedging. The Group does not hold or issue financial         作對沖目的。本集團未持有或發行作交易用途的衍生金融
derivatives for trading purpose.                                                      工具。

the Group’s interest-bearing bank and other borrowings. The Group does not            其他借款有關。本集團並無使用衍生金融工具對沖利率風
use financial derivatives to hedge interest rate risk, and uses variable rate bank    險，並使用浮息銀行借款及其他借款管理其利息成本。
borrowings and other borrowings to manage its interest cost.

Credit Risk                                                                           信貸風險
The Group divides financial instruments on basis of shared credit risk                本 集 團 基 於 共 同 信 貸 風 險 特 徵（比 如 工 具 類 型 及 信 貸 風 險
characteristics, such as instrument type and credit risk ratings for the              等級）對金融工具進行分類，以釐定信貸風險大幅增加及計
purpose of determining significant increases in credit risk and calculation           量 減 值。為 管 理 貿 易 應 收 款 項 產 生 的 風 險，本 集 團 已 制 定
of impairment. To manage risk arising from trade receivables, the Group has           政策確保僅向具備適當信貸歷史的對手方授出信貸期，且
policies in place to ensure that credit terms are made only to counterparties         管理層將對本集團的對手方持續進行信貸評估。客戶的信
with an appropriate credit history and management performs ongoing                    貸質量乃經考慮其財務狀況、過往經驗及其他因素而進行
credit evaluations of the Group’s counterparties. The credit quality of these         評估。本集團亦已擁有其他監控程序以確保落實跟進行動
customers is assessed after taking into account their financial position,             以 收 回 逾 期 應 收 款 項。此 外，本 集 團 定 期 檢 討 貿 易 應 收 款
past experience and other factors. The Group also has other monitoring                項的可收回金額，以確保就不可收回金額計提適當的減值
procedures to ensure that follow-up action is taken to recover overdue                虧 損。本 集 團 並 無 高 度 集 中 的 信 貸 風 險，且 其 信 貸 風 險 分
receivables. In addition, the Group regularly reviews the recoverable amount          散至大量對手方及客戶。
of trade receivables to ensure that adequate impairment losses are made
for irrecoverable amounts. The Group has no significant concentrations of
credit risk, with credit risk spread over a large number of counterparties and
customers.

     creditors, material uncertainties exist as to whether management of the               否實現上述計劃及措施仍存在重大不確定因素。
     Company will be able to achieve its plans and measures as described above.

     Should the going concern assumption be inappropriate, adjustments may                 倘 持 續 經 營 假 設 不 適 當，可 能 須 作 出 調 整，撇 減 資 產 價 值

CHAIRMAN AND CHIEF EXECUTIVE OFFICER                                                主席及行政總裁
CG Code provision C.2.1 stipulates that the roles of the chairman and chief         企業管治守則條文第C.2.1規定主席與行政總裁之角色應分
executive officer should be separate and should not be performed by the             開，並 由 不 同 人 士 擔 任。林 定 强 先 生 為 董 事 會 主 席 兼 本 公
same individual. Mr. Lam Ting Keung is the chairman of the Board and the            司行政總裁。鑒於林先生自本集團成立以來一直承擔營運
chief executive officer of the Company. In view of the fact that Mr. Lam has        及管理本集團的日常責任，董事會相信林定强先生同時承
been taking up day-to-day responsibilities in operating and managing the            擔有效管理及業務發展的職責符合本集團的最佳利益。因
Group since its establishment, the Board believes that it is in the best interest   此，董 事 認 為 偏 離 企 業 管 治 守 則 條 文 第C.2.1條 在 有 關 情 況
of the Group to have Mr. Lam Ting Keung taking up both roles for effective          下乃屬恰當。
management and business development. Therefore, the Directors consider
that the deviation from CG Code provision CS.2.1 is appropriate in such
circumstance.

Notwithstanding the above, the Board views that such management structure           儘管有上述情況，但董事會認為有關管理層架構對本集團
is effective for the Group’s operations and sufficient checks and balances are      營運而言乃屬有效且有足夠的制衡。
in place.

FINANCIAL REPORTING SYSTEM, RISK                                                財務匯報系統、風險管理及內部控制系統
MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL SYSTEM
Financial reporting system                                                      財務匯報系統
The Board, supported by the finance department, is responsible for the          董事會負責在財務部門的支持下編製本公司及本集團的財
preparation of the financial statements of the Company and the Group.           務 報 表。董 事 已 使 用 持 續 經 營 基 準 編 製 財 務 報 表。本 集 團
The directors prepared the financial statements on a going concern basis,       已採納國際財務報告準則並貫徹使用及應用適當的會計政

compliance and the confirmation provided by the Controlling Shareholders          書，作為年度審閱過程的一部分。鑑於：(i)本公司已接獲控
as part of the annual review process. On the basis that: (i) the Company has      股股東有關承諾的確認書；(ii)控股股東並無申報競爭業務；
received the confirmations from the Controlling Shareholders regarding            及(iii)概無特別情況致令全面遵守承諾存疑，獨立非執行董
the Undertakings; (ii) there was no competing business reported by the            事認為，本公司已按照條款遵守及執行承諾。
Controlling Shareholders; and (iii) there was no particular situation rendering
the full compliance of the Undertakings being questionable, the independent
non-executive Directors are of the view that the Undertakings have been
complied with and been enforced by the Company in accordance with the
terms.


SHARE OPTION SCHEME                                                               購股權計劃
Details of the Share Option Scheme                                                購股權計劃的詳情
1)  Purpose                                                                       1) 目的
      The Share Option Scheme is a share incentive scheme established                  購 股 權 計 劃 為 一 項 股 份 獎 勵 計 劃，旨 在 認 可 及 嘉 許
      to recognise and acknowledge the contributions that the Eligible                 合資格參與者（定義見下文(2)段）對本集團作出或可
      Participants (as defined in paragraph (2) below) had or may have                 能 已 作 出 的 貢 獻。購 股 權 計 劃 將 向 合 資 格 參 與 者 提
      made to our Group. The Share Option Scheme will provide the Eligible             供機會於本公司擁有個人權益，以達致下列目標：
      Participants an opportunity to have a personal stake in our Company
      with the view to achieving the following objectives:

      (i)    motivate the Eligible Participants to optimize their performance          (i)    激勵合資格參與者為本集團的利益而優化其表
             efficiency for the benefit of our Group; and                                     現效率；及

      (ii)   attract and retain or otherwise maintain an on-going business             (ii)   吸引及挽留合資格參與者或以其他方式與該等
             relationship with the Eligible Participants whose contributions                  合資格參與者保持持續業務關係，而該等合資
             are or will be beneficial to the long-term growth of our Group.                  格參與者的貢獻乃對或將會對本集團的長遠發
                                                                                              展有利。

     業的公平值乃使用收益法釐定，方法是通過計入以現有租賃所得
     及╱或在現有市場上可收取的物業租金收入，且已就租賃的復歸
     收入潛力作適當估量，並已按適當的資本化率進行資本化以釐定
     公平值。在建商業物業的公平值使用成本法釐定，該方法已計及
     土地使用權的市場價值加上迄今產生的建築成本，並應用適當的
     毛利率。該等假設的變化將對投資物業的估值產生重大影響。因
     此，投資物業的估值被確定為關鍵審核事項。

that includes our opinion. Our report is made solely to you, as a body, and for     外，本 報 告 並 無 其 他 用 途。吾 等 不 會 就 核 數 師 報 告 的 內 容
no other purpose. We do not assume responsibility towards or accept liability       向任何其他人士負上或承擔任何責任。
to any other person for the contents of this report.

    “Group”) for the year ended 31 December 2024. A subsidiary is an                 司 指 本 公 司 直 接 或 間 接 控 制 的 實 體。當 本 集 團 就 參
    entity, directly or indirectly, controlled by the Company. Control is            與投資對象業務而承擔可變回報風險或享有可變回
    achieved when the Group is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns           報權利以及能透過對投資對象行使權力（即現有的可
    from its involvement with the investee and has the ability to affect             讓本集團有能力主導投資對象相關活動的權利）影響
    those returns through its power over the investee (i.e., existing rights         該等回報時，即取得控制權。
    that give the Group the current ability to direct the relevant activities of
    the investee).

    Generally, there is a presumption that a majority of voting rights results       一般而言，過半數投票權被假定為控制權。倘本公司
    in control. When the Company has less than a majority of the voting              擁 有 投 資 對 象 半 數 以 下 的 投 票 權 或 類 似 權 利，則 本
    or similar rights of an investee, the Group considers all relevant facts         集團於評估其是否可對投資對象行使權力時會考慮
    and circumstances in assessing whether it has power over an investee,            一切相關事實及情況，包括：
    including:

    (a)    the contractual arrangement with the other vote holders of the            (a)   與投資對象其他投票持有人訂立的合同安排；
           investee;

    (b)    rights arising from other contractual arrangements; and                   (b)   根據其他合同安排享有的權利；及

    (c)    the Group’s voting rights and potential voting rights.                    (c)   本集團的投票權及潛在投票權。

    The financial statements of the subsidiaries are prepared for the same           附屬公司的財務報表在相同報告期間採用與本公司

    The results of subsidiaries are consolidated from the date on which the          合 併 附 屬 公 司 的 業 績，並 繼 續 合 併 直 至 該 控 制 權 終
    Group obtains control, and continue to be consolidated until the date            止之日。
    that such control ceases.

    controlling interests, even if this results in the non-controlling interests     權 益 有 赤 字 結 餘。本 集 團 成 員 公 司 間 交 易 產 生 的 所
    having a deficit balance. All intra-group assets and liabilities, equity,        有 集 團 內 公 司 間 資 產 及 負 債、權 益、收 入、開 支 和 現

    assets (including goodwill), liabilities, any non-controlling interest           關資產（包括商譽）、負債、任何非控股權益以及匯兌
    and the exchange fluctuation reserve; and recognises the fair value of           波 動 儲 備；並 於 損 益 中 確 認 任 何 保 留 投 資 的 公 平 值

           The nature and impact of the revised IFRS Accounting Standards are               經修訂國際財務報告準則會計準則的性質及影響列
           described below:                                                                 述如下：

           a)    Amendments to IFRS 16 specify the requirements that a seller-              a)   國際財務報告準則第16號（修訂本）訂明賣方－
                 lessee uses in measuring the lease liability arising in a sale                  承租人於計量售後租回交易產生的租賃負債時
                 and leaseback transaction to ensure the seller-lessee does                      使用的規定，以確保賣方－承租人不會確認與
                 not recognise any amount of the gain or loss that relates to                    其所保留使用權有關的任何收益或虧損金額。
                 the right of use it retains. Since the Group has no sale and                    由於本集團自首次應用國際財務報告準則第16
                 leaseback transactions with variable lease payments that do not                 號之日起並無發生包含非取決於指數或利率的
                 depend on an index or a rate occurring from the date of initial                 可變租賃付款的售後租回交易，故該等修訂對
                 application of IFRS 16, the amendments did not have any impact                  本集團的財務狀況或表現並無任何影響。
                 on the financial position or performance of the Group.

           b)    The 2020 Amendments clarify the requirements for classifying               b)   2020年修訂本澄將負債分類為流動或非流動的
                 liabilities as current or non-current, including what is meant by               規 定，包 括 延 期 結 算 權 利 的 含 義，以 及 延 期 權
                 a right to defer settlement and that a right to defer must exist                利必須在報告期末存在。負債的分類不受實體
                 at the end of the reporting period. Classification of a liability is            行使其延期結算權利的可能性所影響。該等修
                 unaffected by the likelihood that the entity will exercise its right            訂 亦 澄 清，負 債 可 以 其 本 身 的 權 益 工 具 結 算，
                 to defer settlement. The amendments also clarify that a liability               且僅在可換股負債之換股權本身作為權益工
                 can be settled in its own equity instruments, and that only if a                具入賬的情況下，負債的條款方不會影響其分
                 conversion option in a convertible liability is itself accounted for            類。2022年 修 訂 本 進 一 步 澄 清，在 貸 款 安 排 產
                 as an equity instrument would the terms of a liability not impact               生的負債契諾中，僅實體於報告日期或之前必
                 its classification. The 2022 Amendments further clarify that,                   須遵守的契諾會影響該負債分類為流動或非流
                 among covenants of a liability arising from a loan arrangement,                 動。凡屬實體須於報告期後12個月內遵守未來
                 only those with which an entity must comply on or before the                    契諾的非流動負債，須作出額外披露。
                 reporting date affect the classification of that liability as current
                 or non-current. Additional disclosures are required for non-
                 current liabilities that are subject to the entity complying with

      2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                                2.4 重要會計政策
           Investments in associates and joint ventures                                 於聯營公司及合營企業的投資
           An associate is an entity in which the Group has a long term interest        聯 營 公 司 為 本 集 團 於 其 一 般 不 少 於20%股 本 投 票 權
           of generally not less than 20% of the equity voting rights and over          中擁有長期權益的實體，且可對其發揮重大影響力。
           which it has significant influence. Significant influence is the power       重大影響力指參與投資對象的財務和經營決策的權
           to participate in the financial and operating policy decisions of the        力，但不是控制或共同控制該等決策的權力。
           investee, but is not control or joint control over those policies.

           A joint venture is a type of joint arrangement whereby the parties that      合 營 企 業 指 一 種 合 營 安 排，對 安 排 擁 有 共 同 控 制 權
           have joint control of the arrangement have rights to the net assets of       的 訂 約 方 據 此 對 合 營 企 業 的 資 產 淨 值 擁 有 權 利。共
           the joint venture. Joint control is the contractually agreed sharing of      同 控 制 指 按 照 合 約 協 定 對 一 項 安 排 所 共 有 的 控 制，
           control of an arrangement, which exists only when decisions about the        共同控制僅在有關活動要求享有控制權的訂約方作
           relevant activities require the unanimous consent of the parties sharing     出一致同意的決定時存在。
           control.

           The Group’s investments in associates and joint ventures are stated          本集團於聯營公司及合營企業的投資乃按本集團根
           in the consolidated statement of financial position at the Group’s           據權益會計法應佔資產淨值減任何減值虧損於綜合
           share of net assets under the equity method of accounting, less              財務狀況表列賬。倘出現任何不相符的會計政策，即
           any impairment losses. Adjustments are made to bring into line any           會 作 出 調 整 加 以 修 正。本 集 團 應 佔 聯 營 公 司 及 合 營
           dissimilar accounting policies that may exist. The Group’s share of the      企業收購後業績及其他全面收益計入綜合損益及其
           post-acquisition results and other comprehensive income of associates        他全面收益表。此外，倘於聯營公司或合營企業的權
           and joint ventures is included in the consolidated statement of profit       益 直 接 確 認 變 動，則 本 集 團 會 於 綜 合 權 益 變 動 表 確
           or loss and other comprehensive income. In addition, when there has          認其應佔任何變動（倘適用）         。本集團與其聯營公司或
           been a change recognised directly in the equity of the associate or          合營企業間交易的未變現收益及虧損將以本集團於
           joint venture, the Group recognises its share of any changes, when           聯 營 公 司 或 合 營 企 業 的 投 資 為 限 對 銷，惟 倘 未 變 現
           applicable, in the consolidated statement of changes in equity.              虧 損 證 明 所 轉 讓 資 產 減 值 則 除 外。收 購 聯 營 公 司 或
           Unrealised gains and losses resulting from transactions between the          合營企業所產生的商譽計入本集團於聯營公司或合
           Group and its associates or joint ventures are eliminated to the extent      營企業的投資內作為其中一部分。
           of the Group’s investments in the associates or joint ventures, except
           where unrealised losses provide evidence of an impairment of the
           assets transferred. Goodwill arising from the acquisition of associates
           or joint ventures is included as part of the Group’s investments in
           associates or joint ventures.

           If an investment in an associate becomes an investment in a joint            倘於聯營公司的投資變成於合營企業的投資或出現
           venture or vice versa, the retained interest is not remeasured. Instead,     相 反 情 況，則 不 會 重 新 計 量 保 留 權 益。反 之，該 投 資
           the investment continues to be accounted for under the equity                繼 續 根 據 權 益 法 入 賬。在 所 有 其 他 情 況 下，失 去 對
           method. In all other cases, upon loss of significant influence over the      聯營公司的重大影響力或對合營企業的共同控制權
           associate or joint control over the joint venture, the Group measures        後，本集團按其公平值計量及確認任何保留投資。聯
           and recognises any retained investment at its fair value. Any difference     營公司或合營企業於失去重大影響力或共同控制權
           between the carrying amount of the associate or joint venture upon           時的賬面值與保留投資及出售所得款項的公平值之
           loss of significant influence or joint control and the fair value of the     間的任何差額乃於損益內確認。
           retained investment and proceeds from disposal is recognised in profit
           or loss.

          When an investment in an associate or a joint venture is classified as        當 聯 營 公 司 或 合 營 企 業 的 投 資 歸 類 為 持 作 出 售 時，
          held for sale, it is accounted for in accordance with IFRS 5 Non-current      則按國際財務報告準則第5號持作出售非流動資產及
          Assets Held for Sale and Discontinued Operations .                            已終止經營業務 入賬。

2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES (Continued)                                       2.4 重要會計政策（續）
    Fair value measurement                                                           公平值計量
    The Group measures its investment properties at the end of each                  本 集 團 於 各 報 告 期 末 投 資 物 業。公 平 值 乃 在 市 場 參
    reporting period. Fair value is the price that would be received to              與者於計量日期進行的有序交易中出售資產所收取
    sell an asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction          或 轉 移 負 債 所 支 付 的 價 格。公 平 值 計 量 乃 基 於 假 設
    between market participants at the measurement date. The fair value              出售資產或轉移負債的交易於資產或負債的主要市
    measurement is based on the presumption that the transaction to                  場 或 於 未 有 主 要 市 場 的 情 況 下，則 於 資 產 或 負 債 的
    sell the asset or transfer the liability takes place either in the principal     最 有 利 市 場 進 行。主 要 或 最 有 利 市 場 須 位 於 本 集 團
    market for the asset or liability, or in the absence of a principal              能 到 達 的 地 方。資 產 或 負 債 的 公 平 值 乃 基 於 市 場 參
    market, in the most advantageous market for the asset or liability. The          與者為資產或負債定價所用的假設計量（假設市場參
    principal or the most advantageous market must be accessible by the              與者依照彼等的最佳經濟利益行事）            。
    Group. The fair value of an asset or a liability is measured using the
    assumptions that market participants would use when pricing the asset
    or liability, assuming that market participants act in their economic
    best interest.

    A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into account             非金融資產公平值的計量則參考市場參與者可從使
    a market participant’s ability to generate economic benefits by using            用 該 資 產 得 到 的 最 高 及 最 佳 效 用，或 把 該 資 產 售 予
    the asset in its highest and best use or by selling it to another market         另一可從使用該資產得到最高及最佳效用的市場參
    participant that would use the asset in its highest and best use.                與者所產生的經濟效益。

    The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in the                  本 集 團 使 用 適 用 於 不 同 情 況 的 估 值 方 法，而 其 有 足
    circumstances and for which sufficient data are available to measure             夠 資 料 計 量 公 平 值，以 盡 量 利 用 相 關 可 觀 察 輸 入 數
    fair value, maximising the use of relevant observable inputs and                 據及盡量減少使用不可觀察輸入數據。
    minimising the use of unobservable inputs.

    All assets and liabilities for which fair value is measured or disclosed in      於財務報表中計量或披露公平值的所有資產及負
    the financial statements are categorised within the fair value hierarchy,        債，均 根 據 對 公 平 值 計 量 整 體 而 言 屬 重 大 的 最 低 級
    described as follows, based on the lowest level input that is significant        別輸入數據在下述公平值層級內進行分類：
    to the fair value measurement as a whole:

    Level 1       –    based on quoted prices (unadjusted) in active                 第一層級       － 基於相同資產或負債於活躍市場的
                       markets for identical assets or liabilities                                所報價格（未經調整）

    Level 2       –    based on valuation techniques for which the                   第二層級       － 基於對公平值計量而言屬重大的可
                       lowest level input that is significant to the fair                         觀 察（直 接 或 間 接）最 低 級 別 輸 入 數
                       value measurement is observable, either directly or                        據的估值方法
                       indirectly

    Level 3       –    based on valuation techniques for which the                   第三層級       － 基於對公平值計量而言屬重大的不
                       lowest level input that is significant to the fair value                   可觀察最低級別輸入數據的估值方
                       measurement is unobservable                                                法

    For assets and liabilities that are recognised in the financial statements       就按經常基準於財務報表中確認的資產及負債而
    on a recurring basis, the Group determines whether transfers have                言，本 集 團 於 各 報 告 期 末 會 重 新 評 估 分 類 方 法（根
    occurred between levels in the hierarchy by reassessing categorisation           據對整體公平值計量而言屬重大的最低層級輸入數
    (based on the lowest level input that is significant to the fair value           據）
                                                                                      ，以釐定轉撥是否已於各層級之間發生。
    measurement as a whole) at the end of each reporting period.

           Impairment of non-financial assets                                               非 金融資產減值
           Where an indication of impairment exists, or when annual impairment              倘 存 在 任 何 減 值 跡 象，或 當 須 每 年 就 資 產 進 行 減 值
           testing for an asset is required (other than inventories, properties under       檢測（存貨、開發中物業、持作出售竣工物業、遞延稅
           development, completed properties held for sale, deferred tax assets,            項資產、金融資產、投資物業除外），則會估計資產的
           financial assets and investment properties), the asset’s recoverable             可 收 回 數 額。資 產 的 可 收 回 數 額 乃 按 資 產 或 現 金 產
           amount is estimated. An asset’s recoverable amount is the higher of the          生單位的使用價值或公平值減出售成本兩者的較高
           asset’s or cash-generating unit’s value in use and its fair value less costs     者計算，而個別資產須分開計算，除非資產並不產生
           of disposal, and is determined for an individual asset, unless the asset         明 顯 獨 立 於 其 他 資 產 或 資 產 組 別 的 現 金 流 入，於 此
           does not generate cash inflows that are largely independent of those             情 況 下，則 可 收 回 數 額 按 資 產 所 屬 現 金 產 生 單 位 的
           from other assets or groups of assets, in which case the recoverable             可收回數額計算。
           amount is determined for the cash-generating unit to which the asset
           belongs.

           In testing a cash-generating unit for impairment, a portion of the               對現金產生單位進行減值測試時，公司資產（如總部
           carrying amount of a corporate asset (e.g., a headquarters building) is          樓 宇）的 部 分 賬 面 值 倘 可 以 按 合 理、一 致 基 準 分 配，
           allocated to an individual cash-generating unit if it can be allocated on        則 分 配 至 單 個 現 金 產 生 單 位，否 則 分 配 至 最 小 一 組
           a reasonable and consistent basis or, otherwise, to the smallest group           現金產生單位。
           of cash-generating units.

           An impairment loss is recognised only if the carrying amount of an               僅 在 資 產 賬 面 值 高 於 其 可 收 回 數 額 的 情 況 下，方 會
           asset exceeds its recoverable amount. In assessing value in use, the             確認減值虧損。評估使用價值時，估計日後現金流量
           estimated future cash flows are discounted to their present value using          按可反映幣值時間值及資產特定風險的現時市場評
           a pre-tax discount rate that reflects current market assessments of the          估 的 稅 前 貼 現 率 貼 現 至 現 值。減 值 虧 損 按 與 該 減 值
           time value of money and the risks specific to the asset. An impairment           資產功能相符的開支類別於產生期間自損益中扣除。
           loss is charged to profit or loss in the period in which it arises in those
           expense categories consistent with the function of the impaired asset.

           An assessment is made at the end of each reporting period as to                  本集團會在各報告期末評估是否有任何跡象顯示
           whether there is an indication that previously recognised impairment             以 前 所 確 認 的 減 值 虧 損 已 不 在 或 可 能 減 少。倘 出 現
           losses may no longer exist or may have decreased. If such an indication          此等跡象，則會估計可收回金額。僅當用以確定資產
           exists, the recoverable amount is estimated. A previously recognised             （商 譽 除 外）可 收 回 金 額 的 估 計 有 變 時，方 會 撥 回 先
           impairment loss of an asset other than goodwill is reversed only if there        前 確 認 的 減 值 虧 損，但 撥 回 後 的 數 額 不 得 超 逾 假 設
           has been a change in the estimates used to determine the recoverable             於過往年度並無就該項資產確認減值虧損而應釐定
           amount of that asset, but not to an amount higher than the carrying              的賬面值（扣除任何折舊╱攤銷）           。減值虧損撥回會計
           amount that would have been determined (net of any depreciation/                 入產生期間的損益。
           amortisation) had no impairment loss been recognised for the asset in
           prior years. A reversal of such an impairment loss is credited to profit or
           loss in the period in which it arises.

2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES (Continued)                                        2.4 重要會計政策（續）
    Related parties                                                                   關聯方
    A party is considered to be related to the Group if:                              以下人士被視為本集團的關聯方，倘：

    (a)   the party is a person or a close member of that person’s family             (a)   有關方為一名人士或該人士家庭的緊密家庭成
          and that person                                                                   員，而該人士

          (i)      has control or joint control over the Group;                             (i)      擁有本集團的控制權或共同控制權；

          (ii)     has significant influence over the Group; or                             (ii)     對本集團產生重大的影響力；或

          (iii)    is a member of the key management personnel of the                       (iii)    為本集團或本集團母公司的主要管理人
                   Group or of a parent of the Group;                                                員的其中一名成員；

    or                                                                                或

    (b)   the party is an entity where any of the following conditions                (b)   該人士為實體且符合下列任何一項條件，而：
          applies:

          (i)      the entity and the Group are members of the same group;                  (i)      該實體與本集團屬同一集團的成員公

           Properties under development                                                   開發中物業

           comprising land costs, construction costs, borrowing costs, professional       本 包 括 土 地 成 本、建 築 成 本、借 款 成 本、專 業 費 用 及

           during the development period and net realisable value.

           unless those will not be realised in the normal operating cycle. On            相 關 開 發 中 物 業 分 類 為 流 動 資 產。物 業 於 完 工 時 轉

           held for sale.

                the lease at the present value of lease payments to be made                   的 現 值 計 量 租 賃 負 債。租 賃 付 款 包 括 固 定 付
                over the lease term. The lease payments include fixed payments                款（包 括 實 物 固 定 付 款）減 去 任 何 應 收 租 賃 優
                (including in-substance fixed payments) less any lease incentives             惠、取 決 於 指 數 或 利 率 的 可 變 租 賃 付 款，以 及
                receivable, variable lease payments that depend on an index or                預期在剩餘價值擔保下支付的金額。租賃付款
                a rate, and amounts expected to be paid under residual value                  亦包括本集團合理地肯定行使的購買選擇權的

                Group and payments of penalties for termination of a lease,                   於觸發付款的事件或條件發生時，不依賴於指
                if the lease term reflects the Group exercising the option to                 數或利率的可變租賃付款將於該期間確認為支
                terminate the lease. The variable lease payments that do not                  出。
                depend on an index or a rate are recognised as an expense in
                the period in which the event or condition that triggers the
                payment occurs.

                In calculating the present value of lease payments, the Group                 在計算租賃付款的現值時，由於租賃中所隱含
                uses its incremental borrowing rate at the lease commencement                 的利率不易確定，則本集團在租賃開始日期使
                date because the interest rate implicit in the lease is not readily           用 累 計 的 借 款 利 率。在 開 始 日 期 之 後，租 賃 負
                determinable. After the commencement date, the amount of                      債的金額將會增加，以反映利息的增加及減少
                lease liabilities is increased to reflect the accretion of interest           租 賃 付 款。此 外，如 有 修 改、租 賃 期 限 發 生 變
                and reduced for the lease payments made. In addition, the                     化、租 賃 付 款 變 化（例 如 因 指 數 或 利 率 變 化 導
                carrying amount of lease liabilities is remeasured if there is a              致的租賃付款變更）或相關資產的購買選擇權
                modification, a change in the lease term, a change in lease                   的評估變更，租賃負債的賬面值將重新計量。
                payments (e.g., a change to future lease payments resulting from
                a change in an index or rate) or a change in assessment of an
                option to purchase the underlying asset.

          (c)   Short-term leases and leases of low-value assets                        (c)   短期租賃及低價值資產租賃
                The Group applies the short-term lease recognition exemption                  本集團將短期租賃確認豁免適用於其辦公室及
                to its short-term leases of office and equipment (that is those               設備的短期租賃，即自開始日期起計之租期為
                leases that have a lease term of 12 months or less from the                   十二個月或以下並且不包括購買選擇權的租
                commencement date and do not contain a purchase option). It                   賃。確認低價值資產的租賃豁免亦適用於被認
                also applies the recognition exemption for leases of low-value                為低價值的辦公設備及筆記型電腦的租賃。
                assets to leases of office equipment and laptop computers that
                are considered to be of low value.

                Lease payments on short-term leases and leases of low-value                   短期租賃的租賃付款及低價值資產租賃在租賃
                assets are recognised as an expense on a straight-line basis over             期內按直線法確認為支出。
                the lease term.

    as operating leases. When a contract contains lease and non-lease              租 賃 及 非 租 賃 部 分 時，本 集 團 根 據 其 相 對 獨 立 出 售

    and is included in revenue in the consolidated statement of profit or          租賃時產生的初始直接成本乃計入租賃資產的賬面

   Investments and other financial assets                                          投資及其他金融資產
   Initial recognition and measurement                                             初始確認及計量
    Financial assets are classified, at initial recognition, as subsequently       金融資產於初始確認時分類為其後按攤銷成本以及
    measured at amortised cost and fair value through profit or loss.              按公平值計入損益計量。

    The classification of financial assets at initial recognition depends          於 初 始 確 認 時，金 融 資 產 分 類 取 決 於 金 融 資 產 的 合
    on the financial asset’s contractual cash flow characteristics and the         約現金流量特點及本集團管理該等金融資產的業務
    Group’s business model for managing them. With the exception                   模 式。除 並 無 重 大 融 資 成 分 或 本 集 團 已 應 用 可 行 權
    of trade receivables that do not contain a significant financing               宜方法（即不調整重大融資成分的影響）的貿易應收
    component or for which the Group has applied the practical expedient           款項外，本集團初步按公平值（倘金融資產並非按公
    of not adjusting the effect of a significant financing component,              平值計入損益）另加交易成本計量金融資產。並無重
    the Group initially measures a financial asset at its fair value, plus in      大融資成分或本集團已應用可行權宜方法的貿易應
    the case of a financial asset not at fair value through profit or loss,        收款項根據下文「收益確認」所載的政策按國際財務
    transaction costs. Trade receivables that do not contain a significant         報告準則第15號釐定的交易價格計量。
    financing component or for which the Group has applied the practical
    expedient are measured at the transaction price determined under IFRS
    15 in accordance with the policies set out for “Revenue recognition”
    below.

    In order for a financial asset to be classified and measured at amortised      為使金融資產按攤銷成本或按公平值計入其他全面
    cost or fair value through other comprehensive income, it needs to             收 益 進 行 分 類 及 計 量，需 產 生 純 粹 為 支 付 本 金 及 未
    give rise to cash flows that are solely payments of principal and interest     償還本金利息（「純粹為支付本金及利息」）的現金流
    (“SPPI”) on the principal amount outstanding. Financial assets with            量。現 金 流 並 非 純 粹 為 支 付 本 金 及 利 息 的 金 融 資 產
    cash flows that are not SPPI are classified and measured at fair value         則按公平值計入損益進行分類及計量且不論業務模
    through profit or loss, irrespective of the business model.                    式。

    A financial asset (or, where applicable, a part of a financial asset or part       金融資產（或（如適用）金融資產的部分或同類金融資

    of the asset. When it has neither transferred nor retained substantially           留 該 資 產 絕 大 部 分 風 險 及 回 報，亦 無 轉 讓 資 產 的 控

    Impairment of financial assets                                                     金 融資產減值
    The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit losses (“ECLs”)              本集團就並非按公平值計入損益持有的所有債務工
    for all debt instruments not held at fair value through profit or loss.            具確認預期信貸虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）撥備。預期信
    ECLs are based on the difference between the contractual cash                      貸虧損乃基於根據合約應付的合約現金流量與本集
    flows due in accordance with the contract and all the cash flows                   團 預 期 收 取 的 所 有 現 金 流 量 之 間 的 差 額 釐 定，並 按
    that the Group expects to receive, discounted at an approximation                  接 近 原 有 實 際 利 率 的 比 率 貼 現。預 期 現 金 流 量 將 包
    of the original effective interest rate. The expected cash flows will              括出售所持抵押品或構成合約條款的其他信貸提升
    include cash flows from the sale of collateral held or other credit                措施所得的現金流量。
    enhancements that are integral to the contractual terms.

   General approach                                                                    一般方法
    ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures for which there            預 期 信 貸 虧 損 分 兩 個 階 段 確 認。就 自 初 始 確 認 以 來
    has not been a significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition,      信貸風險並無大幅增加的信貸風險而言，會就未來12
    ECLs are provided for credit losses that result from default events that           個月可能發生的違約事件所產生的信貸虧損計提預
    are possible within the next 12 months (a 12-month ECL). For those                 期 信 貸 虧 損 撥 備（ 12個 月 預 期 信 貸 虧 損）。就 自 初 始
    credit exposures for which there has been a significant increase in                確 認 以 來 信 貸 風 險 大 幅 增 加 的 信 貸 風 險 而 言，須 就
    credit risk since initial recognition, a loss allowance is required for credit     預期於風險餘下存續期內產生的信貸虧損計提虧損
    losses expected over the remaining life of the exposure, irrespective of           撥備，不論違約的時間（整個存續期預期信貸虧損）                。
    the timing of the default (a lifetime ECL).

           At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether the credit risk on a         於 各 報 告 日 期，本 集 團 評 估 金 融 工 具 的 信 貸 風 險 自
           financial instrument has increased significantly since initial recognition.     初始確認以來是否顯著增加。作此評估時，本集團比
           When making the assessment, the Group compares the risk of a default            較金融工具於報告日期出現違約的風險與該金融工
           occurring on the financial instrument as at the reporting date with             具 於 初 始 確 認 日 期 出 現 違 約 的 風 險，並 考 慮 無 須 花
           the risk of a default occurring on the financial instrument as at the           費不必要成本或精力即可獲得的合理及有理據的資
           date of initial recognition and considers reasonable and supportable            料，包括過往及前瞻性資料。倘合約付款逾期超過30
           information that is available without undue cost or effort, including           日，本集團會視其信貸風險已大幅增加。
           historical and forward-looking information. The Group considers that
           there has been a significant increase in credit risk when contractual
           payments are more than 30 days past due.

           default when internal or external information indicates that the Group          集 團 持 有 的 任 何 信 貸 提 升 措 施 前，本 集 團 不 大 可 能
           is unlikely to receive the outstanding contractual amounts in full before       悉 數 收 取 未 償 還 合 約 款 項，則 本 集 團 亦 可 認 為 金 融
           taking into account any credit enhancements held by the Group. A                資產違約。倘若無法合理預期收回合約現金流量，則
           financial asset is written off when there is no reasonable expectation of       撇銷金融資產。
           recovering the contractual cash flows.

           general approach and they are classified within the following stages            分 類 至 以 下 階 段 以 計 量 預 期 信 貸 虧 損，惟 下 文 所 述

                              increased significantly since initial recognition and                   加，且其虧損撥備等於12個月預期信

   Impairment of financial assets (Continued)                                        金 融資產減值（續）
   Simplified approach                                                               簡化方法
    For trade receivables that do not contain a significant financing                並無重大融資成分或本集團應用可行權宜方法（即不
    component or when the Group applies the practical expedient of                   調整重大融資成分的影響）的貿易應收款項，本集團
    not adjusting the effect of a significant financing component, the               應用簡化方法計算預期信貸虧損。簡化方法下，本集
    Group applies the simplified approach in calculating ECLs. Under the             團 並 無 追 蹤 信 貸 風 險 的 變 化，但 於 各 報 告 日 期 根 據
    simplified approach, the Group does not track changes in credit risk,            整 個 存 續 期 預 期 信 貸 虧 損 確 認 虧 損 撥 備。本 集 團 已
    but instead recognises a loss allowance based on lifetime ECLs at each           根據其以往信貸虧損經驗，建立了撥備矩陣，並就債
    reporting date. The Group has established a provision matrix that is             務人及經濟環境的特定前瞻性因素作出調整。
    based on its historical credit loss experience, adjusted for forward-
    looking factors specific to the debtors and the economic environment.

    For trade receivables that contain a significant financing component             就包含重大融資成分的貿易應收款項及租賃應收款
    and lease receivables, the Group chooses as its accounting policy to             項 而 言，本 集 團 在 計 算 預 期 信 貸 虧 損 時 選 擇 採 納 上
    adopt the simplified approach in calculating ECLs with policies as               述政策的簡化方法作為其會計政策。
    described above.

   Financial liabilities                                                             金融負債
   Initial recognition and measurement                                               初始確認及計量
    Financial liabilities are classified, at initial recognition, as interest-       金融負債於初始確認時分類為計息銀行及其他借
    bearing bank and other borrowings, senior notes, corporate bonds, or             款、優先票據、公司債務及應付款項（如適用）

    payables, as appropriate.

    All financial liabilities are recognised initially at fair value and, in the     所 有 金 融 負 債 均 按 公 平 值 進 行 初 始 確 認，對 於 貸 款
    case of loans and borrowings and payables, net of directly attributable          及借款以及應付款項，則扣除直接應佔交易成本。
    transaction costs.

    The Group’s financial liabilities include interest-bearing bank and other        本 集 團 的 金 融 負 債 包 括 計 息 銀 行 及 其 他 借 款、公 司
    borrowings, corporate bonds and senior notes (collectively called                債券及優先票據（統稱為「貸款及借款」）、租賃負債、
    “loans and borrowings”), lease liabilities, trade payables, other payables       貿 易 應 付 款 項、其 他 應 付 款 項 及 應 計 費 用 及 應 付 關
    and accruals, and amounts due to related companies.                              連公司款項。

   Subsequent measurement                                                            後續計量
    The subsequent measurement of financial liabilities depends on their             金融負債隨後視乎其分類按以下方式計量：
    classification as follows:

   Financial liabilities at amortised cost (trade and other                          按攤銷成本列賬的金融負債（貿易及其他應
   payables, and borrowings)                                                         付款項及借款）
    After initial recognition, trade and other payables, and interest-               於 初 始 確 認 後，貿 易 及 其 他 應 付 款 項 及 計 息 借 款 及
    bearing borrowings and lease liabilities are subsequently measured               租 賃 負 債 其 後 使 用 實 際 利 率 法 按 攤 銷 成 本 計 量，但
    at amortised cost, using the effective interest rate method unless the           於 貼 現 影 響 不 大 的 情 況 下 則 按 成 本 列 賬。收 益 及 虧
    effect of discounting would be immaterial, in which case they are                損在終止確認負債時及於攤銷過程中以實際利率法

    the liabilities are derecognised as well as through the effective interest
    rate amortisation process.

    Amortised cost is calculated by taking into account any discount or              計算攤銷成本時，計及購買時的任何折讓或溢價，以
    premium on acquisition and fees or costs that are an integral part of            及 視 為 實 際 利 率 一 部 分 的 費 用 或 成 本。按 實 際 利 率
    the effective interest rate. The effective interest rate amortisation is         法計算的攤銷計入綜合損益及其他全面收入表的財

    and other comprehensive income.

           A financial liability is derecognised when the obligation under the              金 融 負 債 於 負 債 責 任 獲 解 除、取 消 或 到 期 時 終 止 確
           liability is discharged or cancelled, or expires.                                認。

           of a new liability, and the difference between the respective carrying

    constructive) has arisen as a result of a past event and it is probable        定），而 履 行 該 責 任 可 能 導 致 未 來 資 源 流 出，且 該 責

    obligation, provided that a reliable estimate can be made of the

    reimbursement is virtually certain. The expense relating to a provision        撥備有關的開支在扣除任何補償後於綜合損益及其

    provision is the present value at the end of the reporting period of the       期 末 預 期 須 用 作 履 行 責 任 的 未 來 開 支 的 現 值。因 時

    assets are expected to be settled or recovered.

    Government grants                                                               政府補助
    Government grants are recognised at their fair value where there is             倘能合理確定將會收取政府補助且符合所有附帶條
    reasonable assurance that the grant will be received and all attaching          件，則有關補助將按公平值予以確認。倘補助與開支
    conditions will be complied with. When the grant relates to an expense          項 目 有 關，即 於 擬 補 償 成 本 的 支 銷 期 間 內 系 統 地 確
    item, it is recognised as income on a systematic basis over the periods         認為收入。
    that the costs, for which it is intended to compensate, are expensed.

    Where the grant relates to an asset, the fair value is credited to a            倘補助與資產有關，公平值將計入遞延收入賬戶，並
    deferred income account and is released to profit or loss over the              於 有 關 資 產 的 預 期 可 使 用 年 期 內，以 等 額 年 金 調 撥
    expected useful life of the relevant asset by equal annual instalments or       至損益或從資產賬面值中扣減並以經扣減折舊開支
    deducted from the carrying amount of the asset and released to profit           調撥至損益。
    or loss by way of a reduced depreciation charge.

   Revenue recognition                                                              收益確認
   Revenue from contracts with customers                                            客戶合約收益
    Revenue from contracts with customers is recognised when control                客戶合約收入於貨品或服務的控制權轉移予客戶時
    of goods or services is transferred to the customers at an amount that          予 以 確 認，而 該 金 額 反 映 本 集 團 預 期 就 提 供 該 等 貨
    reflects the consideration to which the Group expects to be entitled in         品或服務有權獲得的代價。
    exchange for those goods or services.

    When the consideration in a contract includes a variable amount,                當 合 約 中 的 代 價 包 括 可 變 金 額 時，估 計 代 價 為 本 集
    the amount of consideration is estimated to which the Group will                團 將 貨 品 或 服 務 轉 移 予 客 戶 而 有 權 獲 得 的 金 額。可
    be entitled in exchange for transferring the goods or services to the           變 代 價 在 合 約 開 始 時 作 出 估 計 並 受 其 約 束，直 至 與
    customer. The variable consideration is estimated at contract inception         可 變 代 價 相 關 的 不 確 定 性 消 除，累 計 已 確 認 收 入 金
    and constrained until it is highly probable that a significant revenue          額極有可能不會發生重大收入轉回時。
    reversal in the amount of cumulative revenue recognised will not occur
    when the associated uncertainty with the variable consideration is
    subsequently resolved.

    Contract liabilities                                                            合約負債
    A contract liability is recognised when a payment is received or a              倘客戶於本集團將貨品或服務轉讓予客戶前支付代
    payment is due (whichever is earlier) from a customer before the                價，則於作出付款或付款到期時（以較早者為準）確認
    Group transfers the related goods or services. Contract liabilities are         合約負債。合約負債於本集團履行合約時確認為收入
    recognised as revenue when the Group performs under the contract                （即相關貨品或服務的控制權轉讓予客戶）。
    (i.e., transfers control of the related goods or services to the customer).

    Contract cost assets                                                            合約成本資產
    Other than the costs which are capitalised as inventories, property,            除資本化為存貨、物業、廠房及設備以及無形資產的
    plant and equipment and intangible assets, costs incurred to fulfil             成本外，倘符合所有下列條件，則將履行客戶合約產
    a contract with a customer are capitalised as an asset if all of the            生的成本資本化為資產：
    following criteria are met:

    (a)    The costs relate directly to a contract or to an anticipated             (a)   有關成本與實體可明確識別之合約或預期合約
           contract that the entity can specifically identify.                            有直接關係。

    (b)    The costs generate or enhance resources of the entity that will          (b)   有關成本令實體將用於履行（或持續履行）日後
           be used in satisfying (or in continuing to satisfy) performance                履約責任之資源得以產生或有所增加。
           obligations in the future.

    (c)    The costs are expected to be recovered.                                  (c)   有關成本預期可收回。

    The capitalised contract cost assets are amortised and charged to profit        資本化合約成本資產乃按與轉讓資產相關貨品或服
    or loss on a systematic basis that is consistent with the transfer to the       務 予 客 戶 一 致 的 系 統 基 準 攤 銷 並 自 損 益 扣 除。其 他
    customer of the goods or services to which the asset related. Other             合約成本於產生時支銷。
    contract costs are expensed as incurred.

           incremental costs for disposing of the asset. When value in use                  理層須估計資產或現金產生單位的預期未來現金流
           calculations are undertaken, management must estimate the expected               量，並 選 擇 適 當 的 貼 現 率 以 計 算 該 等 現 金 流 量 的 現

           a suitable discount rate to calculate the present value of those cash
           flows.

           PRC corporate income tax (“CIT”)                                                 中國企業所得稅（「企業所得稅」）
           The Group is subject to corporate income taxes in the PRC. As a result           本 集 團 須 繳 納 中 國 企 業 所 得 稅。由 於 有 關 所 得 稅 的
           of the fact that certain matters relating to the income taxes have               若 干 事 宜 尚 未 被 地 方 稅 務 局 確 認，於 釐 定 所 作 出 的
           not been confirmed by the local tax bureau, objective estimate and               所 得 稅 撥 備 時 須 以 目 前 有 效 的 稅 法、法 規 及 其 他 有
           judgement based on currently enacted tax laws, regulations and other             關 政 策 作 為 客 觀 估 計 及 判 斷 依 據。倘 該 等 事 宜 的 最
           related policies are required in determining the provision for income            終 稅 款 數 額 有 別 於 原 本 記 錄 的 數 額，差 額 會 在 所 發
           taxes to be made. Where the final tax outcome of these matters is                生的期間對所得稅及稅項撥備產生影響。
           different from the amounts originally recorded, the differences will
           impact on the income tax and tax provisions in the period in which the
           differences realise.

           PRC land appreciation tax (“LAT”)                                                中國土地增值稅（「土地增值稅」）
           The Group is subject to LAT in the PRC. The provision for LAT is based           本 集 團 須 繳 納 中 國 土 地 增 值 稅。土 地 增 值 稅 撥 備 乃
           on management’s best estimates according to the understanding of                 根據管理層按照中國相關稅法及法規所載規定的理
           the requirements set forth in the relevant PRC tax laws and regulations.         解，從而作出的最佳估計計提。實際的土地增值稅負
           The actual LAT liabilities are subject to the determination by the tax           債，須待物業開發項目完成後由稅務當局釐定。本集
           authorities upon the completion of the property development projects.            團尚未就其若干物業開發項目與稅務當局最終確定
           The Group has not finalised its LAT calculation and payments with the            土 地 增 值 稅 的 計 算 及 付 款。最 終 結 果 可 能 與 初 步 入
           tax authorities for certain of its property development projects. The            賬 的 金 額 有 所 不 同，而 任 何 差 額 會 在 所 發 生 的 期 間
           final outcome could be different from the amounts that were initially            對土地增值稅開支及相關撥備產生影響。
           recorded, and any differences will impact on the LAT expenses and the

           Estimate of fair value of investment properties                                  投資物業的公平值估計
           Investment properties carried at fair value were revalued at each                按公平值列賬的投資物業於各報告日期根據獨立專
           reporting date based on the appraised market value provided by                   業 估 值 師 提 供 的 評 估 市 值 重 新 估 值。該 等 估 值 乃 基
           independent professional valuers. Such valuations were based on                  於若干假設作出，存在不確定性，可能與實際結果存
           certain assumptions, which are subject to uncertainty and might                  在重大差異。進行估計時，本集團考慮活躍市場上類
           materially differ from the actual results. In making the estimation, the         似 物 業 的 近 期 價 格 信 息，並 使 用 主 要 基 於 各 年 末 的
           Group considers information from current prices in an active market              市況作出的假設進行估計。
           for similar properties and uses assumptions that are mainly based on
           market conditions existing at the end of each year.

14. INVESTMENT PROPERTIES (Continued)                                            14. 投資物業（續）
    Fair value hierarchy (Continued)                                               公平值層級（續）
    The fair value of completed commercial properties is determined using          已 竣工商業物業的公平值乃使用收益法釐定。
    the income approach.

    The income approach by taking into account the rental income of                收益方法是通過計入以現有租賃所得及╱或在現有
    the properties derived from the existing leases and/or achievable in           市 場 上 可 收 取 的 物 業 租 金 收 入，且 已 就 租 賃 的 復 歸
    the existing market with due allowance for the reversionary income             收 入 潛 力 作 適 當 估 量，並 已 按 適 當 的 資 本 化 率 進 行
    potential of the leases, which have been then capitalised to determine         資本化以釐定公平值。在適當情況下，亦曾考慮參考
    the fair value at an appropriate capitalisation rate. Where appropriate,       相關市場可資比較的銷售交易。
    reference to the comparable sales transactions as available in the
    relevant market has also been considered.

    A significant increase/decrease in the estimated rental value                  估計租金價值大幅增加╱減少將導致投資物業的公
    would result in a significant increase/decrease in the fair value of           平值大幅增加╱減少。資本化率大幅增加／減少將導
    the investment properties. A significant increase/decrease in the              致投資物業的公平值大幅減少╱增加。
    capitalisation rate would result in a significant decrease/increase in the
    fair value of the investment properties.

    The fair value of commercial properties under construction which are           在建商業物業的公平值於建設初期使用資產基礎法
    at the initial construction stage is determined using the asset-based          及剩餘法釐定。
    method and residual method.

    The asset-based method is to use the market comparison method to               資產基礎法是使用市場比較法來評估土地使用權的
    evaluate the fair value of the land use right and consider the book            公 平 值，並 考 慮 該 建 設 及 安 裝 產 生 成 本 的 賬 面 值 以
    value of the construction and installation costs incurred to determine         釐定在建項目的公平值。
    the fair value of the project under construction.

    The residual method is based on the assumption that the property is            剩餘法乃基於該物業為根據最新發展建議（在物業用
    newly completed in accordance with the latest development proposal             途、相 關 可 出 售 面 積 及 建 築 時 間 表 方 面）新 近 落 成，
    in terms of property uses, respective saleable areas and construction          以確立發展總值（「發展總值」）。總開發成本（包括建
    schedules to establish the gross development value (“GDV”). The total          築成本、或然成本、專業費用、基建成本、管理成本、
    development costs including construction costs, contingency costs,             財務成本及發展商溢利）乃估計得出，並扣除已確立
    professional fees, infrastructure costs, management costs, financial           的 發 展 總 值。隨 後 將 所 得 出 的 剩 餘 數 字 調 整 回 至 估
    costs and developer’s profit are estimated and deducted from the               值日期，以得出相關物業權益的市值。收入資本化法
    established GDV. The resultant residual figure is then adjusted back           用 於 估 計 發 展 總 值，即 來 自 該 物 業 的 估 計 收 入 淨 額
    to the valuation date to arrive at the market value of the property            的資本化基準，並考慮現行市場收益率。
    interest concerned. The income capitalisation method has been used in
    estimating the GDV, the basis of capitalisation of estimated net income
    derived from the property with consideration of the prevailing market
    yields.

    A significant increase/decrease in the expected profit margin would            預期利潤的大幅增加╱減少將導致投資物業的公平
    result in a significant decrease/increase in the fair value of the             值大幅減少╱增加。
    investment properties.

36. RESERVES                                                                   36. 儲備
    The amounts of the Group’s reserves and the movements therein for            截至2024年12月31日止年度，本集團的儲備金額及其
    the year ended 31 December 2024 are presented in the consolidated            變動於綜合權益變動表呈列。
    statement of changes in equity.

    (a)   Share premium                                                          (a)   股份溢價
          The share premium represents the difference between the par                  股份溢價指已發行股份面值與所收代價之間的
          value of the shares issued and the consideration received.                   差額。

    (b)   Capital reserve                                                        (b)   資本儲備
          The capital reserve represents any difference between the                    資本儲備指非控股權益獲調整的金額與收購附
          amount by which the non-controlling interests are adjusted and               屬公司的非控股權益所付代價的公平值之間的
          the fair value of the consideration paid for acquisition of non-             任何差額。資本儲備的變動詳情載於綜合權益
          controlling interests in subsidiaries. Details of the movements in           變動表。
          the capital reserve are set out in the consolidated statement of
          changes in equity.

    (c)   Statutory surplus reserve                                              (c)   法定盈餘儲備
          In accordance with the PRC Company Law and the articles                      根據中國公司法及於中國成立的附屬公司的組
          of association of the subsidiaries established in the PRC, the               織 章 程 細 則，本 集 團 須 按 稅 後 溢 利 淨 額 的10%
          Group is required to appropriate 10% of its net profits after                提取法定盈餘儲備，此乃根據中國會計準則釐
          tax, as determined under PRC GAAP, to the statutory surplus                  定，直 至 儲 備 餘 額 達 到 其 註 冊 資 本 的50%。受
          reserve until the reserve balance reaches 50% of its registered              相關中國法規及本集團組織章程細則所載若干
          capital. Subject to certain restrictions set out in the relevant             限制的規限，法定盈餘儲備可用於抵銷虧損或
          PRC regulations and in the articles of association of the Group,             轉換為增加股本，惟轉換後儲備餘額不得少於
          the statutory surplus reserve may be used either to offset                   本 集 團 註 冊 資 本 的25%。儲 備 不 得 用 於 其 設 立
          losses, or to be converted to increase share capital provided                目的以外的其他用途，亦不得作為現金股息分
          that the balance after such conversion is not less than 25% of               派。
          the registered capital of the Group. The reserve cannot be used
          for purposes other than those for which it is created and is not
          distributable as cash dividends.

    (d)   Asset revaluation reserve                                              (d)   資產重估儲備
          The asset revaluation reserve arises from change in use from an              資產重估儲備源於一項自用物業的用途更改為
          owner-occupied property to an investment property.                           投資物業。

                  Group is responsible for repaying the outstanding mortgage principals                罰款。
                  together with any accrued interest and penalties owed by the defaulted
                  purchasers to those banks.

                  to the banks as collateral for the mortgage loans, upon default on                   揭貸款的抵押品；倘該等買方拖欠按揭還款，該等銀
                  mortgage repayments by these purchasers, the banks are entitled to                   行 有 權 接 管 有 關 法 定 業 權，並 透 過 公 開 拍 賣 將 抵 押
                  take over the legal titles and will realise the pledged properties through           物業變現。
                  open auction.

                  The Group’s guarantee period starts from the dates of grant of the                   本集團的擔保期由授出相關按揭貸款日期起至買方
                  relevant mortgage loans and ends upon the issuance and registration                  獲 發 物 業 所 有 權 證 及 辦 理 登 記 止，有 關 證 明 一 般 會
                  of property ownership certificates to the purchasers, which will                     於買方接管相關物業後的一至兩年內取得。
                  generally be available within one to two years after the purchasers take
                  possession of the relevant properties.

                  The Group did not incur any material losses during the year in respect               年 內，本 集 團 就 向 本 集 團 持 作 出 售 的 竣 工 物 業 買 方
                  of the guarantees provided for mortgage facilities granted to purchasers             授出的按揭融資提供的擔保並未產生任何重大損
                  of the Group’s completed properties held for sale. The directors of the              失。本公司董事認為如出現違約付款，相關物業的可
                  Company considered that in the case of default on payments, the net                  變現淨值足以償還未償還按揭貸款連同任何應計利
                  realisable value of the related properties would be sufficient to repay              息及罰款，故並無對擔保計提撥備。
                  the outstanding mortgage loans together with any accrued interest and
                  penalty, and therefore no provision has been made in connection with
                  the guarantees.

           finance for the Group’s operations.

           The main risks arising from the Group’s financial instruments are              來 自 本 集 團 金 融 工 具 的 主 要 風 險 為 利 率 風 險、外 幣
           interest rate risk, foreign currency risk, credit risk and liquidity risk.     風 險、信 貸 風 險 及 流 動 資 金 風 險。通 常，本 集 團 在 風
           Generally, the Group introduces conservative strategies on its risk            險 管 理 方 面 引 入 保 守 的 策 略。本 集 團 未 持 有 或 發 行
           management. The Group does not hold or issue derivative financial              作 交 易 用 途 的 衍 生 金 融 工 具。董 事 會 審 閱 並 同 意 管
           instruments for trading purposes. The board of directors reviews               理各類風險的政策，有關政策概述於下。
           and agrees policies for managing each of these risks and they are

                 set out in note 31. The Group does not use derivative financial                使用衍生金融工具對沖利率風險。本集團使用
                 instruments to hedge interest rate risk. The Group manages its                 浮息銀行借款管理其利息成本。
                 interest cost using variable rate bank borrowings.

           (e)   Capital management                                                   (e)   資本管理
                 The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management are               本集團資本管理的主要目標為保障本集團持續
                 to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern            經營的能力並維持穩健的資本比率，以支持其
                 and to maintain healthy capital ratios in order to support its             業務並使股東價值最大化。
                 business and maximise shareholders’ value.

                 The Group manages its capital structure and makes adjustments              本集團會根據經濟狀況的變化管理及調整其資
                 to it in light of changes in economic conditions. To maintain or           本 結 構。為 維 持 或 調 整 資 本 結 構，本 集 團 或 會
                 adjust the capital structure, the Group may adjust the dividend            調整派付予股東的股息、返還股東資本或發行
                 payment to shareholders, return capital to shareholders or issue           新股。
                 new shares.

                 The Group monitors capital using a net gearing ratio, which                本集團採用資產負債比率（按債務總額減現金
                 is based on total indebtedness less cash and bank balances                 及銀行結餘（包括現金及現金等價物及受限制
                 (include Cash and cash equivalents and Restricted cash) divided            現金），除以年末權益總額再乘以100%計算）來
                 by total equity at the end of the year and multiplied by 100%.             監 控 資 本。債 務 總 額 指 計 息 銀 行 及 其 他 借 款、
                 Total indebtedness represents total interest-bearing bank                  資產抵押證券所得款項、公司債券及優先票據
                 and other borrowings, proceeds from asset-backed securities,               之總額。於各有關期間結束時的資產負債比率
                 corporate bonds and senior notes. The net gearing ratios as at             如下：
